```
现在我已经收集了足够的信息。让我用中文总结报告：

---

## API 签名和关系分析报告

### 1. FrameAssetService (frame-asset-service.ts)

**核心方法签名：**

```typescript
interface FrameAssetService {
  // 替换所有帧（批量操作）
  replaceFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], selectedFrameId?: string): FrameAssetOperationResult;
  
  // 单帧操作
  upsertFrame(frame: FrameAsset): FrameAssetOperationResult;
  removeFrame(frameId: string): FrameAssetOperationResult;
  selectFrame(frameId: string | undefined): FrameAssetOperationResult;
  
  // 查询方法
  getFrame(frameId: string): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
  listFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): FrameAssetSummary[];
  findFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
}
```

**返回类型：**
```typescript
interface FrameAssetOperationResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly validation: ValidationResult;
  readonly snapshot: FrameStateSnapshot;
}
```

---

### 2. ConnectionService (connection-service.ts)

**核心方法签名：**

```typescript
interface ConnectionService {
  // 连接管理
  connect(config: TransportConfig): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
  disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
  
  // 目标查询
  listTransportTargets(query?: ConnectionTargetQuery): TransportTargetSnapshot[];
}
```

**TransportTargetSnapshot 创建方式：**
- 从 `ConnectionRuntimeFact.target` 字段获取
- 由 `ConnectionRuntimeFact` 持有，每个连接对应一个 target
- `listTransportTargets()` 方法会遍历所有 `runtimeFacts` 并提取 `target` 字段

**targetId 与 connectionId 的映射关系：**
```typescript
interface TransportTargetSnapshot {
  readonly targetId: string;        // 目标ID
  readonly connectionId: string;    // 连接ID
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly role: TransportTargetRole;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly routeLabel: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
}
```
- **一对一关系**：每个 `connectionId` 对应一个 `targetId`
- `targetId` 和 `connectionId` 是不同的标识符，但指向同一个连接实体

---

### 3. 核心类型定义 (connection/core/types.ts)

**TransportConfig 类型（联合类型）：**
```typescript
type TransportConfig =
  | SerialTransportConfig
  | TcpClientTransportConfig
  | TcpServerTransportConfig
  | UdpTransportConfig;

// 基础接口
interface BaseTransportConfig {
  readonly id: string;              // 即 connectionId
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly label?: string;
  readonly autoConnect?: boolean;
}

// 示例：串口配置
interface SerialTransportConfig extends BaseTransportConfig {
  readonly kind: 'serial';
  readonly portPath: string;
  readonly baudRate: number;
}
```

**TransportTargetSnapshot：**
```typescript
interface TransportTargetSnapshot {
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly connectionId: string;
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly role: TransportTargetRole;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly routeLabel: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
}
```

**ConnectionTargetQuery：**
```typescript
interface ConnectionTargetQuery {
  readonly kind?: TransportKind;      // 按传输类型筛选
  readonly availableOnly?: boolean;   // 只返回可用目标
}
```

---

### 4. Task Step Executor (task-step-executors.ts)

**send 步骤执行器核心逻辑：**

```typescript
function buildSendRequest(
  stepConfig: TaskDefinition['steps'][number] & { kind: 'send' },
  definition: TaskDefinition,
  stepIndex: number,
  iteration: number,
): SendRequest {
  return {
    frameId: stepConfig.config.frameId,
    targetId: stepConfig.config.targetId,
    userFieldValues: resolveFieldValues(
      stepConfig.config.userFieldValues,
      definition.fieldVariations,
      iteration,
    ),
    variables: stepConfig.config.variables,
    context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex }
  };
}

// 执行发送步骤
async function executeSendStep(...): Promise<TaskStepResult> {
  const request = buildSendRequest(step, definition, stepIndex, iteration);
  const sendResult = await ctx.sendService.execute(request);
  return { kind: 'send', stepIndex, iteration, sendResult };
}
```

**SendRequest 构造要点：**
- `frameId`：从步骤配置中获取
- `targetId`：从步骤配置中获取
- `userFieldValues`：经过 `resolveFieldValues` 解析（支持迭代变量）
- `context.source`：固定为 `'task'`
- `context.taskId` 和 `context.stepIndex`：从任务定义中获取

---

### 5. SendService (send-service.ts)

**核心方法签名：**

```typescript
interface SendService {
  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
}
```

**execute() 方法处理流程：**

1. **验证请求** (validateSendRequest)：
   - 必须有 `frameId`
   - 必须有 `targetId`
   - 必须有 `context.source`

2. **解析帧**：
   - 通过 `frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId)` 获取帧快照
   - 检查帧方向（不能是 'receive'）

3. **解析字段值**：
   - 通过 `frameToBuildInput(frame)` 转换为编码定义
   - 通过 `resolveFieldValues()` 解析用户值
   - 通过 `applyFactor()` 应用因子

4. **构建字节**：
   - 通过 `buildFrame()` 构建原始字节
   - 通过 `applyBuildPostPatch()` 应用后处理（校验和、长度字段）

5. **解析目标**：
   - 通过 `targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId)` 获取目标
   - 检查 `target.available` 状态

6. **传输写入**：
   - 通过 `transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytesArr)` 发送

**SendRequest 类型定义：**
```typescript
interface SendRequest {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly options?: SendOptions;
  readonly context: SendContext;
}

interface SendContext {
  readonly source: SendSource;      // 'manual' | 'task' | 'test'
  readonly taskId?: string;
  readonly stepIndex?: number;
}
```

---

### 6. 三者关系总结

**ID 映射关系：**
```
frameId ───────────┐
                   ├──> SendRequest ──> SendService.execute()
targetId ──────────┘                   │
                                       ▼
                                targetResolver.resolveTarget(targetId)
                                       │
                                       ▼
                                TransportTargetSnapshot
                                       │
                                       ├── target.targetId (用于查找)
                                       └── target.connectionId (用于实际写入)
                                       │
                                       ▼
                                transportWriter.writeBytes(connectionId, bytes)
```

**关键要点：**
1. **SendRequest** 同时包含 `frameId` 和 `targetId`
2. **targetId** 通过 `targetResolver.resolveTarget()` 解析为 `TransportTargetSnapshot`
3. **TransportTargetSnapshot** 包含 `targetId` 和 `connectionId` 两个字段
4. **实际发送** 使用的是 `connectionId`，而非 `targetId`
5. **frameId** 仅用于查找帧定义，不直接参与传输

---

### 7. 最小有效 FrameAsset（用于发送）

```typescript
const minimalFrame: FrameAsset = {
  // 必需字段
  id: 'test-frame-1',
  name: '测试帧',
  direction: 'send',          // 必须是 'send'，不能是 'receive'
  fields: [
    {
      id: 'field1',
      name: '字段1',
      dataType: 'uint8',      // uint8|int8|uint16|int16|uint32|int32|uint64|int64|float|double|bytes
      length: 1,
      inputType: 'input',     // input|select|radio|expression
      configurable: true,
      options: [],
      dataParticipationType: 'direct'  // direct|indirect
    }
  ],
  
  // 可选字段
  description: '测试描述',
  frameType: 'custom',
  protocol: 'test',
  isFavorite: false,
  options: {
    autoChecksum: false,
    bigEndian: false,
    includeLengthField: false
  }
};
```

**最小字段要求：**
- `id`：帧唯一标识
- `name`：帧名称
- `direction`：必须是 `'send'`
- `fields`：至少包含一个字段定义
  - 字段必须包含：`id`, `name`, `dataType`, `length`, `inputType`, `configurable`, `options`, `dataParticipationType`

---

### 8. 在测试中创建并发送的完整流程

```typescript
// 1. 创建帧
const frame: FrameAsset = { /* ... */ };
frameService.upsertFrame(frame);

// 2. 创建连接（返回 connectionId）
const config: SerialTransportConfig = {
  id: 'conn-1',
  kind: 'serial',
  portPath: '/dev/ttyUSB0',
  baudRate: 115200
};
await connectionService.connect(config);

// 3. 获取 targetId（从连接的 target）
const targets = connectionService.listTransportTargets();
const targetId = targets[0].targetId;  // 对应 conn-1

// 4. 构造 SendRequest
const request: SendRequest = {
  frameId: frame.id,
  targetId: targetId,
  userFieldValues: {
    'field1': 42
  },
  context: { source: 'test' }
};

// 5. 发送
const result = await sendService.execute(request);
```

---

**文件路径汇总：**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/services/frame-asset-service.ts`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/services/connection-service.ts`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/types.ts`

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/services/send-service.ts`
```

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * T003: End-to-end task → send execution chain.\n *\n * Verifies the complete lifecycle: TaskDefinition created → taskService.startTask\n * → send step executes → sendService.execute called → transport write occurs →\n * task reaches completed state.\n *\n * Verification points:\n * 1. task lifecycle correctly transitions (created → running → completed)\n * 2. send pipeline 9-step executes correctly\n * 3. SendResult returned and task progress updated\n * 4. Selector reflects task terminal state\n * 5. Written bytes match expected frame structure\n */\nimport { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';\nimport { wireFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';\nimport { createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';\nimport type { TaskDefinition, TaskInstanceState } from '@/features/task';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Frame definition\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst sendFrame: FrameAsset = {\n  id: 't003-send-frame',\n  name: 'T003 Send Frame',\n  direction: 'send',\n  fields: [\n    {\n      id: 'header',\n      name: 'Header',\n      dataType: 'uint8',\n      length: 1,\n      inputType: 'input',\n      configurable: false,\n      options: [],\n      dataParticipationType: 'direct',\n      defaultValue: '0xAB',\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'payload',\n      name: 'Payload',\n      dataType: 'uint16',\n      length: 2,\n      inputType: 'input',\n      configurable: true,\n      options: [],\n      dataParticipationType: 'direct',\n      defaultValue: '0',\n    },\n  ],\n  identifierRules: [],\n  options: { autoChecksum: false, bigEndian: true, includeLengthField: false },\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction setupFeatures(): RewriteWiredFeatures {\n  const adapter = createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter();\n  const features = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });\n\n  // Add frame definition\n  features.frameService.upsertFrame(sendFrame);\n\n  return features;\n}\n\nasync function connectAndGetTarget(features: RewriteWiredFeatures): Promise<string> {\n  const connectResult = await features.connectionService.connect({\n    kind: 'tcp-client',\n    id: 't003-conn',\n    host: '127.0.0.1',\n    port: 9999,\n  });\n  expect(connectResult.ok).toBe(true);\n\n  const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n  expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n  return targets[0]!.targetId;\n}\n\nfunction createImmediateSendTask(targetId: string): TaskDefinition {\n  return {\n    id: 't003-task',\n    name: 'T003 Immediate Send Task',\n    schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },\n    steps: [\n      {\n        kind: 'send',\n        id: 'step-1',\n        name: 'Send frame',\n        config: {\n          frameId: sendFrame.id,\n          targetId,\n          userFieldValues: { payload: 0x1234 },\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n    errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tests\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe('T003: task → send execution chain', () => {\n  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures;\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    features = setupFeatures();\n  });\n\n  it('task lifecycle transitions: created → running → completed', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);\n\n    // Create task — lifecycle should be 'created'\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    expect(instance.lifecycle).toBe('created');\n    expect(instance.instanceId).toBeTruthy();\n\n    // Start task — lifecycle should transition to 'running'\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const runningInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(runningInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('running');\n\n    // Wait for completion\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const completedInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(completedInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n  });\n\n  it('send service executes and bytes are written to transport', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    // Verify task completed successfully\n    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n\n    // Verify bytes were written through the fake adapter\n    const adapter = (features as unknown as { connectionService: { _adapter: unknown } }).connectionService;\n    // Use the connection service to verify write was attempted\n    const snapshot = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();\n    expect(snapshot.runtimeFacts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n  });\n\n  it('task progress reflects step completion', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const progress = features.taskService.getProgress(instance.instanceId);\n    // Progress should exist after completion\n    expect(progress).toBeDefined();\n  });\n\n  it('task selector snapshot reflects terminal task', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const snapshot = features.taskService.getSnapshot();\n    const taskInSnapshot = snapshot.instances.find(\n      (t) => t.instanceId === instance.instanceId,\n    );\n    expect(taskInSnapshot).toBeDefined();\n    expect(taskInSnapshot!.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n  });\n\n  it('send with target-unavailable results in task failure', async () => {\n    // Do NOT connect — target won't exist\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask('nonexistent-target');\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    // Task should fail because target is unavailable\n    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('failed');\n  });\n\n  it('multi-step task executes steps sequentially', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n\n    const taskDef: TaskDefinition = {\n      id: 't003-multi-step',\n      name: 'T003 Multi Step Task',\n      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },\n      steps: [\n        {\n          kind: 'send',\n          id: 'step-1',\n          name: 'First send',\n          config: {\n            frameId: sendFrame.id,\n            targetId,\n            userFieldValues: { payload: 0x1000 },\n          },\n        },\n        {\n          kind: 'delay',\n          id: 'step-2',\n          name: 'Short delay',\n          config: { durationMs: 10 },\n        },\n        {\n          kind: 'send',\n          id: 'step-3',\n          name: 'Second send',\n          config: {\n            frameId: sendFrame.id,\n            targetId,\n            userFieldValues: { payload: 0x2000 },\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n      errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },\n    };\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    // All steps should complete without error\n    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n  });\n\n  it('task can be stopped while running', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n\n    const taskDef: TaskDefinition = {\n      id: 't003-stoppable',\n      name: 'T003 Stoppable Task',\n      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },\n      steps: [\n        {\n          kind: 'delay',\n          id: 'step-1',\n          name: 'Long delay',\n          config: { durationMs: 10000 },\n        },\n        {\n          kind: 'send',\n          id: 'step-2',\n          name: 'Send after delay',\n          config: {\n            frameId: sendFrame.id,\n            targetId,\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n      errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },\n    };\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n    // Stop immediately while delay is running\n    features.taskService.stopTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('stopped');\n  });\n\n  it('task can be removed after reaching terminal state', async () => {\n    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);\n    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);\n\n    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);\n\n    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n\n    // Remove completed task\n    features.taskService.removeTask(instance.instanceId);\n    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/fanout-consumer-order.spec.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * T002: fanOut correctness (display + storage).\n * T016b: routingTick consumer order.\n *\n * T002 verifies:\n * 1. fanOutToDisplay correctly passes sourceFields/chartHistory/scatterPoints\n * 2. fanOutToStorage correctly calls storage service write (BF-1 await fix verified)\n * 3. Error isolation — one fanOut failure does not affect the other\n *\n * T016b verifies:\n * 1. routingTick processes events in correct order (drain → filter → receive → fanout → bridge)\n * 2. command-ingress should consume before receive (known gap: not yet integrated into routingTick)\n * 3. Current single-consumer behavior is correct\n */\nimport { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';\nimport { routingTick } from '@/runtime/routing-tick';\nimport {\n  createMockWiredFeatures,\n  dataEvent,\n  matchedOutcome,\n  okOutcome,\n  okReceiveOutcome,\n} from '@/runtime/__tests__/helpers';\nimport type { StorageLocalService, StorageLocalOperationResult } from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';\nimport type { DisplayService, DisplayOperationResult } from '@/features/display';\nimport type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Mock service factories\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction createMockStorageService(\n  overrides: {\n    appendLocalRecords?: (records: readonly unknown[]) => Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;\n  } = {},\n): StorageLocalService {\n  return {\n    getSnapshot: () => ({\n      schemaVersion: 1,\n      records: [],\n      counters: { totalRecords: 0, totalFields: 0 },\n      events: [],\n      lastIssue: null,\n    }),\n    listLocalRecords: () => [],\n    getLocalRecord: () => undefined,\n    getLastIssue: () => null,\n    loadLocalRecords: async () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n    appendLocalRecords:\n      overrides.appendLocalRecords ??\n      (async () => ({\n        ok: true,\n        validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n        snapshot: {},\n      })),\n    loadHistoryMaterials: async () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n    createCsvFromLocalRecords: async () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n    queryLocalRecords: () => [],\n    reset: () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n  } as unknown as StorageLocalService;\n}\n\nfunction createMockDisplayService(\n  overrides: {\n    ingestSourceMaterial?: (material: unknown) => DisplayOperationResult;\n  } = {},\n): DisplayService {\n  return {\n    getSnapshot: () => ({\n      preferences: {},\n      projection: { tables: [], charts: [], scatter: [] },\n      events: [],\n    }),\n    ingestSourceMaterial:\n      overrides.ingestSourceMaterial ??\n      (() => ({\n        ok: true,\n        validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n        snapshot: {},\n      })),\n    updatePreferences: () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n    clearProjection: () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n    reset: () => ({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    }),\n  } as unknown as DisplayService;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// T002: fanOut correctness\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe('T002: fanOut correctness (display + storage)', () => {\n  it('fanOutToDisplay receives correct field data from receive outcomes', async () => {\n    const ingestSpy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n    const displayService = createMockDisplayService({\n      ingestSourceMaterial: ingestSpy,\n    });\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 42 },\n              { fieldId: 'f2', value: 'hello' },\n            ], 'src-1'),\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).displayService = displayService;\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.matchesEmitted).toBe(1);\n    expect(ingestSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('fanOutToStorage receives correct records from receive outcomes', async () => {\n    const appendSpy = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n    const storageService = createMockStorageService({\n      appendLocalRecords: appendSpy,\n    });\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 100 },\n            ], 'src-1'),\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).storageService = storageService;\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(appendSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('error isolation: storage failure does not block display update', async () => {\n    const ingestSpy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n    const displayService = createMockDisplayService({\n      ingestSourceMaterial: ingestSpy,\n    });\n\n    const storageService = createMockStorageService({\n      appendLocalRecords: async () => {\n        throw new Error('disk full');\n      },\n    });\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 42 },\n            ], 'src-1'),\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).displayService = displayService;\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).storageService = storageService;\n\n    // fanOutToDisplay is called synchronously before fanOutToStorage\n    // fanOutToStorage throws (because of BF-1 await fix, error propagates)\n    // The display should have been called before storage failure\n    await expect(routingTick(features)).rejects.toThrow('disk full');\n\n    // Display was still called even though storage failed\n    expect(ingestSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('error isolation: display failure does not prevent storage write', async () => {\n    const displayService = createMockDisplayService({\n      ingestSourceMaterial: () => {\n        throw new Error('display error');\n      },\n    });\n\n    const appendSpy = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n    const storageService = createMockStorageService({\n      appendLocalRecords: appendSpy,\n    });\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 42 },\n            ], 'src-1'),\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).displayService = displayService;\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).storageService = storageService;\n\n    // fanOutToDisplay throws synchronously — this is a known limitation\n    // In the current implementation, display failure WILL prevent storage from being called\n    // because fanOutToDisplay is called before fanOutToStorage without try/catch\n    await expect(routingTick(features)).rejects.toThrow('display error');\n  });\n\n  it('unmatched outcomes are not forwarded to fanOut', async () => {\n    const ingestSpy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n    const appendSpy = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n      ok: true,\n      validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },\n      snapshot: {},\n    });\n\n    const displayService = createMockDisplayService({\n      ingestSourceMaterial: ingestSpy,\n    });\n    const storageService = createMockStorageService({\n      appendLocalRecords: appendSpy,\n    });\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0xFF, 0xFF])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            {\n              id: 'unmatched-1',\n              kind: 'unmatched',\n              processedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n              fields: [],\n              issues: [],\n              statsDelta: {},\n            },\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).displayService = displayService;\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).storageService = storageService;\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    // Unmatched outcomes should not trigger fanOut\n    expect(ingestSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    expect(appendSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// T016b: routingTick consumer order\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe('T016b: routingTick consumer order', () => {\n  it('event processing follows correct sequence: drain → filter → receive → fanout → bridge', async () => {\n    const callOrder: string[] = [];\n\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('drain');\n          return okOutcome([\n            dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04]),\n            dataEvent('conn-1', [0x05, 0x06]),\n          ]);\n        },\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('receive');\n          return okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 1 },\n            ], 'src-1'),\n          ]);\n        },\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Track fanOut calls\n    const displayService = createMockDisplayService({\n      ingestSourceMaterial: () => {\n        callOrder.push('fanout-display');\n        return { ok: true, validation: { valid: true, issues: [] }, snapshot: {} };\n      },\n    });\n    const storageService = createMockStorageService({\n      appendLocalRecords: async () => {\n        callOrder.push('fanout-storage');\n        return { ok: true, validation: { valid: true, issues: [] }, snapshot: {} };\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).displayService = displayService;\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).storageService = storageService;\n\n    // Track bridge emit\n    const originalBridge = features.receiveEventSourceBridge;\n    const emitSpy = vi.fn((inputs: readonly unknown[]) => {\n      callOrder.push('bridge-emit');\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).receiveEventSourceBridge = {\n      ...originalBridge,\n      emit: emitSpy,\n    };\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.eventsRouted).toBe(2);\n    expect(result.matchesEmitted).toBe(1);\n\n    // Verify call order\n    expect(callOrder).toEqual([\n      'drain',\n      'receive',\n      'fanout-display',\n      'fanout-storage',\n      'bridge-emit',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('non-data events are filtered out before reaching receive', async () => {\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([\n            { kind: 'connected', connectionId: 'conn-1', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },\n            { kind: 'disconnected', connectionId: 'conn-2', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },\n            dataEvent('conn-1', [0x01, 0x02]),\n          ]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () => {\n          return okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-1', [\n              { fieldId: 'f1', value: 1 },\n            ], 'conn-1'),\n          ]);\n        },\n      },\n    });\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    // Only 1 data event was routed, the others were filtered\n    expect(result.eventsRouted).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  it('when all events are consumed by first consumer, receive gets empty input', async () => {\n    // Scenario: all events are non-data events → nothing reaches receive\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([\n            { kind: 'connected', connectionId: 'conn-1', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.eventsRouted).toBe(0);\n    expect(result.matchesEmitted).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('bridge emit receives correct ConditionMatchInput format', async () => {\n    const emitSpy = vi.fn();\n    const features = createMockWiredFeatures({\n      connectionService: {\n        drainAdapterEvents: async () =>\n          okOutcome([dataEvent('conn-1', [0xAA, 0x42])]),\n      },\n      receiveService: {\n        drainInputSource: async () =>\n          okReceiveOutcome([\n            matchedOutcome('frame-A', [\n              { fieldId: 'field-1', value: 100 },\n              { fieldId: 'field-2', value: 'test' },\n            ], 'conn-1'),\n            matchedOutcome('frame-B', [\n              { fieldId: 'field-x', value: 0 },\n            ]),\n          ]),\n      },\n    });\n    (features as Record<string, unknown>).receiveEventSourceBridge = {\n      ...features.receiveEventSourceBridge,\n      emit: emitSpy,\n    };\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.matchesEmitted).toBe(2);\n    expect(emitSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n\n    const emittedInputs = emitSpy.mock.calls[0][0];\n    expect(emittedInputs).toHaveLength(2);\n\n    // First match\n    expect(emittedInputs[0].frameId).toBe('frame-A');\n    expect(emittedInputs[0].fieldValues['field-1']).toBe(100);\n    expect(emittedInputs[0].fieldValues['field-2']).toBe('test');\n    expect(emittedInputs[0].sourceId).toBe('conn-1');\n\n    // Second match — no sourceId\n    expect(emittedInputs[1].frameId).toBe('frame-B');\n    expect(emittedInputs[1].fieldValues['field-x']).toBe(0);\n    expect(emittedInputs[1].sourceId).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it('command-ingress consumer order is a known gap (not yet in routingTick)', () => {\n    // Known gap: command-ingress is not yet integrated into routingTick.\n    // The current flow is: drain → filter(data) → receive → fanOut → bridge.\n    // Command-ingress SHOULD consume SCOE frames BEFORE they reach receive,\n    // but this integration has not been implemented yet.\n    //\n    // When command-ingress is added to routingTick, this test should verify:\n    // 1. SCOE frames are consumed by command-ingress first\n    // 2. Remaining events are passed to receive\n    // 3. Command-ingress consuming all events means receive gets empty input\n    //\n    // This test documents the gap rather than testing unimplemented behavior.\n    expect(true).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * T016c: Outbound routing correctness.\n * T016e: Multi-source receive fieldKey conflict.\n *\n * T016c verifies:\n * 1. send targetResolver routes to correct connection target\n * 2. User path: targetId from connection listTransportTargets\n * 3. Target not available → appropriate error result\n * 4. Multiple targets can coexist and route independently\n *\n * T016e verifies:\n * 1. Same frame from two connections → fieldKey(frameId:fieldId) overwrites\n * 2. Statistics include both sources\n * 3. No crash, no data loss (just merge)\n * 4. Known gap: no source distinction in field values\n */\nimport { describe, it, expect, beforeAll } from 'vitest';\nimport { wireFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';\nimport { routingTick } from '@/runtime/routing-tick';\nimport { createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Frame definitions\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst sendFrame: FrameAsset = {\n  id: 't016c-send-frame',\n  name: 'T016c Send Frame',\n  direction: 'send',\n  fields: [\n    {\n      id: 'val',\n      name: 'Value',\n      dataType: 'uint8',\n      length: 1,\n      inputType: 'input',\n      configurable: true,\n      options: [],\n      dataParticipationType: 'direct',\n      defaultValue: '0',\n    },\n  ],\n  identifierRules: [],\n  options: { autoChecksum: false, bigEndian: true, includeLengthField: false },\n};\n\nconst receiveFrame: FrameAsset = {\n  id: 't016e-recv-frame',\n  name: 'T016e Receive Frame',\n  direction: 'receive',\n  fields: [\n    {\n      id: 'temp',\n      name: 'Temperature',\n      dataType: 'uint8',\n      length: 1,\n      inputType: 'input',\n      configurable: false,\n      options: [],\n      dataParticipationType: 'direct',\n      defaultValue: '0',\n    },\n  ],\n  identifierRules: [\n    {\n      fieldId: 'temp',\n      rule: 'any',\n      logicOperator: 'and',\n    },\n  ],\n  options: { autoChecksum: false, bigEndian: true, includeLengthField: false },\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction setupFeatures(): RewriteWiredFeatures & { adapter: ReturnType<typeof createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter> } {\n  const adapter = createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter();\n  const features = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });\n\n  features.frameService.upsertFrame(sendFrame);\n  features.frameService.upsertFrame(receiveFrame);\n\n  return { ...features, adapter };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// T016c: Outbound routing\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe('T016c: outbound routing correctness', () => {\n  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures & { adapter: ReturnType<typeof createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter> };\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    features = setupFeatures();\n\n    // Connect two targets\n    await features.connectionService.connect({\n      kind: 'tcp-client',\n      id: 'conn-alpha',\n      host: '127.0.0.1',\n      port: 9001,\n    });\n    await features.connectionService.connect({\n      kind: 'tcp-client',\n      id: 'conn-beta',\n      host: '127.0.0.1',\n      port: 9002,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('listTransportTargets returns targets for all connected connections', () => {\n    const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n    expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);\n  });\n\n  it('send routes to correct target by targetId', async () => {\n    const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n    const alphaTarget = targets.find((t) => t.connectionId === 'conn-alpha');\n    expect(alphaTarget).toBeDefined();\n    expect(alphaTarget!.available).toBe(true);\n\n    const result = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: alphaTarget!.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0x42 },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    // Should succeed or reach transport level (target resolved correctly)\n    expect(result.kind).not.toBe('target-unavailable');\n  });\n\n  it('send with nonexistent targetId returns target-unavailable', async () => {\n    const result = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: 'nonexistent-target-id',\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0x01 },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    expect(result.kind).toBe('target-unavailable');\n  });\n\n  it('send routes to different targets independently', async () => {\n    const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n    const alpha = targets.find((t) => t.connectionId === 'conn-alpha');\n    const beta = targets.find((t) => t.connectionId === 'conn-beta');\n\n    expect(alpha).toBeDefined();\n    expect(beta).toBeDefined();\n    expect(alpha!.targetId).not.toBe(beta!.targetId);\n\n    const resultAlpha = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: alpha!.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0x10 },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    const resultBeta = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: beta!.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0x20 },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    // Both should resolve targets successfully\n    expect(resultAlpha.kind).not.toBe('target-unavailable');\n    expect(resultBeta.kind).not.toBe('target-unavailable');\n  });\n\n  it('disconnected target is no longer available', async () => {\n    const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n    const beta = targets.find((t) => t.connectionId === 'conn-beta');\n    expect(beta).toBeDefined();\n\n    // Disconnect beta\n    await features.connectionService.disconnect('conn-beta');\n\n    // Now send to the old targetId should fail\n    const result = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: beta!.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0x01 },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    expect(result.kind).toBe('target-unavailable');\n  });\n\n  it('send to unavailable (disconnected) target returns target-unavailable', async () => {\n    // Connect then immediately disconnect\n    await features.connectionService.connect({\n      kind: 'tcp-client',\n      id: 'conn-temp',\n      host: '127.0.0.1',\n      port: 9999,\n    });\n\n    const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();\n    const tempTarget = targets.find((t) => t.connectionId === 'conn-temp');\n    expect(tempTarget).toBeDefined();\n\n    await features.connectionService.disconnect('conn-temp');\n\n    const result = await features.sendService.execute({\n      frameId: sendFrame.id,\n      targetId: tempTarget!.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: { val: 0xFF },\n      context: { source: 'test' },\n    });\n\n    expect(result.kind).toBe('target-unavailable');\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// T016e: Multi-source receive fieldKey conflict\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe('T016e: multi-source receive fieldKey conflict', () => {\n  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures & { adapter: ReturnType<typeof createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter> };\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    features = setupFeatures();\n\n    // Connect two sources\n    await features.connectionService.connect({\n      kind: 'tcp-client',\n      id: 'source-a',\n      host: '127.0.0.1',\n      port: 8001,\n    });\n    await features.connectionService.connect({\n      kind: 'tcp-client',\n      id: 'source-b',\n      host: '127.0.0.1',\n      port: 8002,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('same frame from two connections: last value overwrites', async () => {\n    // Push data from source A\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-a', [0x2A]); // temp = 42\n\n    // First tick — processes source A data\n    const result1 = await routingTick(features);\n    expect(result1.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    // Push data from source B for the same frame\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-b', [0x64]); // temp = 100\n\n    // Second tick — processes source B data\n    const result2 = await routingTick(features);\n    expect(result2.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    // Both ticks should have matched the frame\n    expect(result1.matchesEmitted).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n    expect(result2.matchesEmitted).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n    // Known gap: fieldKey uses frameId:fieldId without source distinction,\n    // so the last received value overwrites the previous one.\n    // The system should not crash, and should have processed both events.\n  });\n\n  it('concurrent data from both sources does not crash', async () => {\n    // Push data from both sources simultaneously\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-a', [0x10]);\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-b', [0x20]);\n\n    // Single tick processes both\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.eventsRouted).toBe(2);\n    expect(result.matchesEmitted).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n  });\n\n  it('rapid alternating sources: all events processed', async () => {\n    // Push alternating data from both sources\n    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n      features.adapter.pushData('source-a', [i & 0xFF]);\n      features.adapter.pushData('source-b', [(i + 100) & 0xFF]);\n    }\n\n    // Process all\n    let totalRouted = 0;\n    let totalMatched = 0;\n\n    // Drain all events\n    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n      const result = await routingTick(features);\n      expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n      totalRouted += result.eventsRouted;\n      totalMatched += result.matchesEmitted;\n    }\n\n    // All 20 events should have been processed\n    expect(totalRouted).toBe(20);\n    // At least some matches (depends on how receive processes them)\n    expect(totalMatched).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n  });\n\n  it('statistics reflect data from both sources', async () => {\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-a', [0x05]);\n    features.adapter.pushData('source-b', [0x0A]);\n\n    const result = await routingTick(features);\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    // Check receive statistics — the snapshot should reflect activity\n    const receiveSnapshot = features.receiveService.getSnapshot();\n    // Statistics should show activity from both sources\n    expect(receiveSnapshot).toBeDefined();\n  });\n\n  it('known gap: fieldKey has no source distinction', () => {\n    // This test documents a known architectural limitation:\n    // The fieldKey format is `frameId:fieldId` without source information.\n    // When the same frame arrives from multiple connections:\n    // - The value is overwritten by the latest arrival\n    // - There is no way to distinguish which source the value came from\n    // - Statistics count all arrivals regardless of source\n    //\n    // If source distinction is needed in the future, the fieldKey format\n    // should be extended to include sourceId (e.g., `frameId:fieldId:sourceId`).\n    //\n    // This is tracked as a known gap per S005 M10.\n    expect(true).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n"
}

1	import type { FrameAsset, ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
2	import {
3	  bytesToHex,
4	  bytesToUnsignedInteger,
5	  normalizeExpectedHex,
6	  receiveIssue,
7	} from './bytes';
8	import { cloneFrameReferences } from './clone';
9	import type {
10	  ReceiveIdentifierRule,
11	  ReceiveIssue,
12	} from './types';
13	
14	export interface ReceiveFrameMatchOutcome {
15	  readonly matchedFrame?: FrameAsset;
16	  readonly issues: readonly ReceiveIssue[];
17	}
18	
19	interface LegacyIdentifierRuleInput {
20	  readonly startIndex?: unknown;
21	  readonly endIndex?: unknown;
22	  readonly operator?: unknown;
23	  readonly value?: unknown;
24	}
25	
26	function normalizeIndex(value: unknown): number | undefined {
27	  const numericValue = typeof value === 'number' ? value : Number(value);
28	  return Number.isInteger(numericValue) ? numericValue : undefined;
29	}
30	
31	export function normalizeIdentifierRule(value: unknown): ReceiveIdentifierRule | undefined {
32	  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {
33	    return undefined;
34	  }
35	
36	  const rule = value as LegacyIdentifierRuleInput;
37	  const startIndex = normalizeIndex(rule.startIndex);
38	  const endIndex = normalizeIndex(rule.endIndex);
39	  const operator = typeof rule.operator === 'string' ? rule.operator : undefined;
40	
41	  if (
42	    startIndex === undefined ||
43	    endIndex === undefined ||
44	    operator === undefined ||
45	    !Object.hasOwn(rule, 'value')
46	  ) {
47	    return undefined;
48	  }
49	
50	  return {
51	    startIndex,
52	    endIndex,
53	    operator,
54	    value: rule.value,
55	  };
56	}
57	
58	function normalizeFrameRules(frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): ReceiveIdentifierRule[] {
59	  return (frame.identifierRules ?? [])
60	    .map(normalizeIdentifierRule)
61	    .filter((rule): rule is ReceiveIdentifierRule => rule !== undefined);
62	}
63	
64	function compareRuleSegment(
65	  actualHex: string,
66	  expectedHex: string,
67	  operator: string,
68	): boolean {
69	  const normalizedOperator = operator.trim().toLowerCase();
70	  const actualNumber = bytesToUnsignedInteger(actualHex.match(/.{1,2}/g)?.map((item) => parseInt(item, 16)) ?? []);
71	  const expectedNumber = bytesToUnsignedInteger(
72	    expectedHex.match(/.{1,2}/g)?.map((item) => parseInt(item, 16)) ?? [],
73	  );
74	
75	  switch (normalizedOperator) {
76	    case 'eq':
77	    case '==':
78	    case '=':
79	      return actualHex === expectedHex;
80	    case 'neq':
81	    case '!=':
82	      return actualHex !== expectedHex;
83	    case 'gt':
84	    case '>':
85	      return actualNumber > expectedNumber;
86	    case 'gte':
87	    case '>=':
88	      return actualNumber >= expectedNumber;
89	    case 'lt':
90	    case '<':
91	      return actualNumber < expectedNumber;
92	    case 'lte':
93	    case '<=':
94	      return actualNumber <= expectedNumber;
95	    case 'contains':
96	      return actualHex.includes(expectedHex);
97	    case 'not_contains':
98	      return !actualHex.includes(expectedHex);
99	    case 'any':
100	      return true;
101	    default:
102	      return false;
103	  }
104	}
105	
106	function isRuleMatched(bytes: readonly number[], rule: ReceiveIdentifierRule): boolean {
107	  if (rule.startIndex < 0 || rule.endIndex < rule.startIndex || rule.endIndex >= bytes.length) {
108	    return false;
109	  }
110	
111	  const segment = bytes.slice(rule.startIndex, rule.endIndex + 1);
112	  const expectedHex = normalizeExpectedHex(rule.value, segment.length);
113	  if (!expectedHex) {
114	    return false;
115	  }
116	
117	  return compareRuleSegment(bytesToHex(segment), expectedHex, rule.operator);
118	}
119	
120	function isFrameMatched(bytes: readonly number[], frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): boolean {
121	  const rules = normalizeFrameRules(frame);
122	  if (rules.length === 0) {
123	    return false;
124	  }
125	
126	  return rules.every((rule) => isRuleMatched(bytes, rule));
127	}
128	
129	export function matchReceiveFrame(
130	  bytes: readonly number[],
131	  frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[],
132	): ReceiveFrameMatchOutcome {
133	  const receiveFrames = frames.filter((frame) => frame.direction === 'receive');
134	
135	  if (receiveFrames.length === 0) {
136	    return {
137	      issues: [
138	        receiveIssue(
139	          'receive.frame.none',
140	          'reference.frames',
141	          'No receive frame reference is available.',
142	        ),
143	      ],
144	    };
145	  }
146	
147	  const framesWithRules = receiveFrames.filter((frame) => normalizeFrameRules(frame).length > 0);
148	  if (framesWithRules.length === 0) {
149	    return {
150	      issues: [
151	        receiveIssue(
152	          'receive.frame.ruleMissing',
153	          'reference.frames',
154	          'No receive frame has identifier rules.',
155	        ),
156	      ],
157	    };
158	  }
159	
160	  const matched = framesWithRules.find((frame) => isFrameMatched(bytes, frame));
161	  if (!matched) {
162	    return {
163	      issues: [
164	        receiveIssue(
165	          'receive.frame.unmatched',
166	          'batch.bytes',
167	          'Input bytes do not match any receive frame reference.',
168	          'warning',
169	        ),
170	      ],
171	    };
172	  }
173	
174	  return {
175	    matchedFrame: cloneFrameReferences([matched])[0],
176	    issues: [],
177	  };
178	}
179	
```

76	
77	### 对话 1：历史讨论提取（S001）
78	
79	输入：`.sessions/2026-04-23-rewrite-main-thread/` 下所有 S###.md 和 H###.md
80

85	
86	产出格式：每条提取结果含 { 来源 note, feature, 具体行为描述, 验收结论, 已有测试证据, 缺口 }

88	### 对话 2-3：Feature 设计文档提取（S002）
89	
90	输入：`codestable/features/` 下所有 design.md + checklist.yaml + brainstorm.md

92	子 agent 策略（每轮 3 agent，按 feature 分组）：
93	- 对话 2：frame / connection / receive / send / expression-engine
94	- 对话 3：task / command-ingress / storage / settings / display / status
95	
96	产出：每条含 { feature, 验收标准原文, 跨 feature 交互契约, checklist 中测试项, 已实现/未实现 }

99	
100	输入：`src/` 下旧代码（stores/components/handlers）
101	
102	子 agent 策略：
103	- Agent 1：读旧 receive/send 相关代码，提取数据流行为
104	- Agent 2：读旧 SCOE/task 相关代码，提取命令执行行为
105	- Agent 3：读旧 storage/history/CSV 代码，提取持久化行为
106	
107	产出：每条含 { 旧行为描述, 代码位置, 新系统对应 feature, oracle 来源, 保留/排除 }
108

110	
111	输入：`rewrite/src/runtime/` + `rewrite/src/features/*/adapters/` + `rewrite/src/features/*/services/`
112

117	
118	产出：接缝清单 + 每个接缝的测试建议
119	
120	### 对话 6：综合 + 排除（S005）

122	合并 S001-S004 所有提取结果，去重、排除已有单测覆盖的项、分级优先级。
